![Black background](https://cdn.sanity.io/images/xpujt61d/production/5bd59f334a14bd40414d9e351fb4c163940cef67-3600x2739.jpg?rect=0,2151,1792,588&w=2400&fm=webp&q=85&dpr=1)

## Our brand manifesto

**We built the machines to do the work.**  
And they did.  
They type, code, design, plan, schedule, optimize, and execute — faster than we ever could.  
AI is becoming our greatest productivity tool.  
Soon it will be all of productivity.

![Focus illustration](https://cdn.sanity.io/images/xpujt61d/production/fc7bfabb14f0000bbb69be0214f2340aacedd399-8192x6144.png?w=640&fm=webp&q=85&dpr=1)

Focus illustration

**Execution will become infinite.**  
The world filled with answers.  
Slides create themselves.  
Campaigns launch themselves.  
Prototypes build themselves.  
The cost of creation falls to zero.  
And with it, so does its meaning.

**The bottleneck moves.**  
Not in how fast we create.  
But in what we choose to create.  
Not in outputs.  
But in intention, taste, and originality.

![Exploring illustration](https://cdn.sanity.io/images/xpujt61d/production/b269afa46d08119c5d904b63b64b1934442e881a-8192x8192.png?w=640&fm=webp&q=85&dpr=1)

Exploring illustration

**Thinking becomes the work.**  
The final human task.  
The last untouched space.  
To frame the right problems.  
To ask the right questions.  
To set the right direction.  
Thinking is no longer preparation for work.  
It is the work.

![Thinker illustration](https://cdn.sanity.io/images/xpujt61d/production/fab7dbd8637760abe72fb69b424d9d880c33173c-8193x8193.png?w=640&fm=webp&q=85&dpr=1)

Thinker illustration

**And so we needed new tools.**  
Not to automate thought.  
Not to predict or distract.  
But to hold it. Shape it. Focus it.  
Not software. Not hardware.  
Instead, a thinking ecosystem.

![Organised illustration](https://cdn.sanity.io/images/xpujt61d/production/e6c927c29a718468be1f221ddffc2430de6fdac5-8192x8192.png?w=640&fm=webp&q=85&dpr=1)

Organised illustration

**This Is what reMarkable is for.**  
Not for your desk,  
but for your mind.  
Not to type faster,  
but to think better.  
Not to escape the age of AI,  
but to amplify what makes us great.

**  
Human, original thought.**

![Capturing thoughts](https://cdn.sanity.io/images/xpujt61d/production/7f212d20c9a9c0a05e9781a3b783d60ca941598e-8192x8192.png?w=640&fm=webp&q=85&dpr=1)

Capturing thoughts
